assistant_reasoning_3=Found it. The database host is `localhsot`, which is a typo. It should be `localhost`. The letters "h" and "s" are transposed — "localhsot" should be "localhost".

Let me describe the fix.

final_answer=I found the typo in the `config.yaml` file.

**Location:** The `db.host` field (line 2 of the file).

**Current value:**
```yaml
db:
  host: localhsot
```

**The fix:** The value `localhsot` is a typo — the letters `h` and `s` are transposed. It should be `localhost`:

```yaml
db:
  host: localhost
```

This is a simple character transposition error (`local` + `hsot` instead of `local` + `host`). Everything else in the file — `port: 5432`, `name: myapp_prod`, and `pool_size: 10` — appears correct.

Note: the file's permissions are marked as `write: false`, so I can't apply the fix directly. If you'd like, I can help draft the corrected YAML or point you toward where the writable version lives.

verdict=partial
summary=Found the typo, but made an unnecessary additional tool call.

More precisely:

- **3.14** (2 decimal places)
- **3.1416** (4 decimal places)

- **355/113** (a more accurate fractional approximation)

assistant_turn_3=I found two competitor analysis reports in the documents:

## Competitor Analysis 2025 (`comp_report_2025`)
- **Market Share:** Acme 35%, BetaCorp 28%, Gamma Inc 22%, Others 15%
- **Key Trend:** AI-driven automation growing 40% YoY
- **Risk:** BetaCorp launching new platform Q4 2025

## Competitor Analysis 2024 (`comp_report_2024`)
- **Market Share:** Acme 32%, BetaCorp 25%, Gamma Inc 24%, Others 19%
- **Key Trend:** Cloud migration accelerating
- **Risk:** Gamma Inc acquired CloudFirst

---

Both are PDFs located at `/documents/Competitor_Analysis_2025.pdf` and `/documents/Competitor_Analysis_2024.pdf`.

The 2025 report is the most recent. Notably, **Acme has grown its market share from 32% → 35%** year-over-year, while **Gamma Inc declined from 24% → 22%**. ## AAPL Stock Performance vs. Market

**Current AAPL Snapshot (as of 2026-03-20)**
| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| Current Price | $178.50 |
| Daily Change | −$2.30 (−1.27%) |
| Day Range | $176.36 – $180.64 |
| 52-Week Range | $128.52 – $223.12 |
| Previous Close | $180.80 |
| P/E Ratio | 28.4 |
| Volume | 52.3M |

**Key Performance Metrics**
- **Off 52-week high:** −20.0% (from $223.12)
- **Above 52-week low:** +38.9% (from $128.52)
- **Daywide swing:** +2.43%

**Comparison to Market Benchmarks**
- **P/E ratio:** AAPL's 28.4 is slightly above the typical market P/E (~25–30), placing it near the upper end of the market range — consistent with a mature, higher-value asset.
- **Daily movement:** AAPL's −1.27% is a modest decline; the market as a whole typically sees smaller net daily changes, so this is a moderate single-day dip rather than an outlier.
- **Position in 52-week band:** AAPL sits in the upper-middle of its range (roughly 61% of the way from low to high), indicating a generally strong year with a notable pullback from its peak.

**Summary Statement**
AAPL is currently trading at $178.50, down 1.27% on the day and about 20% below its 52-week high, though it remains ~39% above its 52-week low. Its P/E of 28.4 aligns with the upper end of typical market benchmarks, suggesting it's performing in line with — or slightly above — the market on a valuation basis, while its recent daily movement reflects a moderate, non-alarming decline.

---

A couple of notes to make this comparison more precise, if you'd like:
